Portfolio Occupancy Reaches All-time High &#x2013;</font></p><p style="tab-size:26.66px;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Arial;margin-bottom:0;text-align:center;"><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">&#x2013; Raises 2026 Outlook on Growth in Net Income &amp; FFO &#x2013;</font></p><p style="tab-size:26.66px;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Arial;margin-bottom:0;text-align:center;"><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">&#x2013;  Same Property NOI Growth Accelerates &#x2013;</font></p><p style="tab-size:26.66px;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Arial;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p><p style="tab-size:26.66px;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Arial;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p><p style="tab-size:26.66px;font-size:6.7pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Arial;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">JERICHO, New York</font><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">,</font><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;"> October 29, 2026</font><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;"> - Kimco Realty</font><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;vertical-align:super;font-size:6.7pt;line-height:1.5;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">&#174;</font><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;"> (NYSE: KIM), a real estate investment trust (&#x201c;REIT&#x201d;) and leading owner and operator of high-quality, open-air, grocery-anchored shopping centers and mixed-use properties in the United States, today reported results for the third quarter ended September 30, 2026. For the three months ended September 30, 2026 and 2025, Net income available to the company&#x2019;s common shareholders (&#x201c;Net income&#x201d;) per diluted share was $0.41 and $0.19, respectively.</font><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;"> </font></p><p style="tab-size:26.66px;font-size:10pt;margin-top:12pt;font-family:Arial;margin-bottom:6pt;margin-right:1.731%;text-align:justify;"><font Highlights</font><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;"> </font></p><div to $0.47.</font></div></div><div class="item-list-element-wrapper" style="tab-size:32px;display:flex;font-size:10pt;margin-top:3pt;justify-content:flex-start;font-family:Arial;align-items:baseline;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:0.667%;min-width:3.333%;text-align:left;"><font style="tab-size:32px;display:flex;font-size:10pt;margin-top:3pt;justify-content:flex-start;font-family:Arial;align-items:baseline;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:0.667%;min-width:3.333%;text-align:left;"><font leases.</font></div></div><div class="item-list-element-wrapper" style="tab-size:32px;display:flex;font-size:10pt;margin-top:3pt;justify-content:flex-start;font-family:Arial;align-items:baseline;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:0.667%;min-width:3.333%;text-align:left;"><font year-over-year.</font></div></div><div class="item-list-element-wrapper" style="tab-size:32px;display:flex;font-size:10pt;margin-top:3pt;justify-content:flex-start;font-family:Arial;align-items:baseline;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:0.667%;min-width:3.333%;text-align:left;"><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">Repurchased 6.3 million shares of common stock totaling $150 million and repurchased approximately $74 million of preferred stock.</font></div></div><p style="tab-size:26.66px;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Arial;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:0.667%;text-align:left;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p><p style="tab-size:26.66px;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Arial;margin-bottom:0;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">&#34;Our third quarter results reflect strong execution, with record occupancy, significant pricing power on new leases and further acceleration in same property NOI growth. During the quarter, we sold a multifamily property at approximately a 5% cap rate and redeployed the capital toward the repurchase of $150 million of our common stock at an implied cap rate in the mid-7% range, while continuing to recycle lower-growth ground leases into higher-growth grocery-anchored centers,&#34; said Conor Flynn, Kimco CEO. &#34;We still see further opportunity to drive our results higher, including $76 million of annual base rent from leases signed but yet to commence. This positive momentum allowed us to again raise our full year guidance, which would mark the third consecutive year of FFO per share growth above 5%. We remain committed to growing earnings and creating long-term shareholder value.&#34;</font></p><p style="tab-size:26.66px;font-size:10pt;margin-top:18pt;font-family:Arial;margin-bottom:6pt;margin-right:1.731%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;text-decoration:underline solid;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">Financial Results</font></p><p style="tab-size:26.66px;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Arial;margin-bottom:6pt;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">Net income for the third quarter of 2026 was $272.5 million, or $0.41 per diluted share, compared to $130.2 million, or $0.19 per diluted share, for the third quarter of 2025. The year-over-year increase was primarily driven by $122.4 million more of gains on sales of properties, net of impairments.</font><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;"> </font></p><p style="tab-size:26.66px;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Arial;margin-bottom:6pt;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">FFO was $316.2 million, or $0.47 per diluted share, for the third quarter of 2026, compared to $300.3 million, or $0.44 per diluted share, for the third quarter of 2025. Gains on sales of properties, net of impairments, are excluded from the company's calculation of FFO.</font></p><p style="tab-size:26.66px;font-size:10pt;margin-top:12pt;font-family:Arial;margin-bottom:6pt;margin-right:1.731%;text-align:justify;"><font Results</font></p><div class="item-list-element-wrapper" sequentially and 90 basis points year-over-year. </font></div></div><div class="item-list-element-wrapper" style="tab-size:32px;display:flex;font-size:10pt;margin-top:3pt;justify-content:flex-start;font-family:Arial;align-items:baseline;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:0.667%;min-width:3.333%;text-align:justify;"><font basis points year-over-year.</font></div></div></div>

  <div style="padding-top:0.38in;z-index:2;min-height:0.7in;position:relative;box-sizing:border-box;"><p style="tab-size:26.66px;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Times New Roman;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;"><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Times New Roman;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">&#160;</font></p></div>
  <div class="main-content-container" style="z-index:5;position:relative;"><div class="item-list-element-wrapper" style="tab-size:32px;display:flex;font-size:10pt;margin-top:6pt;justify-content:flex-start;font-family:Arial;align-items:baseline;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:0.667%;min-width:3.333%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Times New Roman;font-kerning:none;min-width:3.333%;word-break:keep-all;display:inline-flex;justify-content:flex-start;">&#x2022;</font><div style="display:inline;"><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">Pro-rata anchor occupancy increased 40 basis points sequentially and 120 basis points year-over-year to 98.2%.</font></div></div><div class="item-list-element-wrapper" style="tab-size:32px;display:flex;font-size:10pt;margin-top:3pt;justify-content:flex-start;font-family:Arial;align-items:baseline;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:0.667%;min-width:3.333%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Times New Roman;font-kerning:none;min-width:3.333%;word-break:keep-all;display:inline-flex;justify-content:flex-start;">&#x2022;</font><div style="display:inline;"><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">Generated 4.9% growth in same property NOI year-over-year, driven by a 3.2% increase in minimum rents. Credit loss, as a percentage of total pro-rata rental revenues, was 45 basis points during the quarter.</font></div></div><div class="item-list-element-wrapper" style="tab-size:32px;display:flex;font-size:10pt;margin-top:3pt;justify-content:flex-start;font-family:Arial;align-items:baseline;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:0.667%;min-width:3.333%;text-align:justify;"><font was 390 basis points, a 10 basis point sequential compression, representing $76 million in future rents from signed leases that have not yet commenced.</font></div></div><p Activities</font><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-weight:bold;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;"> </font></p><div class="item-list-element-wrapper" style="tab-size:32px;display:flex;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;justify-content:flex-start;font-family:Arial;align-items:baseline;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:0.667%;min-width:3.333%;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Times New Roman;font-kerning:none;min-width:3.333%;word-break:keep-all;display:inline-flex;justify-content:flex-start;">&#x2022;</font><div style="display:inline;"><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">Sold The Witmer, a 440-unit multifamily building at the company's Pentagon Centre mixed-use property in Pentagon City, Virginia, for $211.5 million. The company&#x2019;s portfolio is strategically concentrated in the first-ring suburbs of the top major metropolitan markets, including high-barrier-to-entry coastal markets and Sun Belt cities. Its tenant mix is focused on essential, necessity-based goods and services that drive multiple shopping trips per week. Publicly traded on the NYSE since 1991 and included in the S&amp;P 500 Index, the company has specialized in shopping center ownership, management, acquisitions, and value-enhancing redevelopment activities for more than 65 years. With a proven commitment to corporate responsibility, Kimco Realty is a recognized industry leader in this area. As of September 30, 2026, the company owned interests in 561 U.S. shopping centers and mixed-use assets comprising 99 million square feet of gross leasable space.</font></p><p style="tab-size:26.66px;font-size:10pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Arial;margin-bottom:6pt;text-align:justify;"><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">The company announces material information to its investors using the company&#x2019;s investor relations website (</font><font><font style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">investors.kimcorealty.com</font></font><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:10pt;font-family:Arial;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">), SEC filings, press releases, public conference calls, and webcasts.       <td style="padding-top:0.01in;padding-left:0in;vertical-align:top;padding-bottom:0in;padding-right:0.01in;"><p style="tab-size:26.66px;font-size:8pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Arial;margin-bottom:0;text-align:center;"><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">(2)</font></p></td>

      <td style="padding-top:0.01in;padding-left:0in;vertical-align:top;padding-bottom:0in;padding-right:0.01in;"><p style="tab-size:26.66px;font-size:8pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Arial;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">Reflects the potential impact if convertible preferred shares and certain units were converted to common stock at the beginning of the period. FFO available to the company&#x2019;s common shareholders would be increased by $1,799 and $2,186 for the three months ended September 30, 2026 and 2025, respectively. FFO available to the company's common shareholders would be increased by $6,353 and $6,233 for the nine months ended September 30, 2026 and 2025, respectively. The effect of other certain convertible units would have an anti-dilutive effect upon the calculation of FFO available to the company&#x2019;s common shareholders per share. Accordingly, the impact of such conversion has not been included in the determination of diluted FFO per share calculations. Additionally, there were potentially issuable shares related to the Exchangeable Senior Notes that were not dilutive as of September 30, 2026.</font></p></td>

     <tr style="height:9pt;background-color:#ffffff;word-break:break-word;white-space:pre-wrap;text-align:left;">
      <td style="padding-top:0.01in;padding-left:0in;vertical-align:top;padding-bottom:0in;padding-right:0.01in;"><p style="tab-size:26.66px;font-size:9pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Arial;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">(1)</font></p></td>
      <td style="padding-top:0.01in;padding-left:0in;vertical-align:top;padding-bottom:0in;padding-right:0.01in;"><p style="tab-size:26.66px;font-size:9pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Arial;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">Same property Net Operating Income (&#x201c;NOI&#x201d;) is a supplemental non-GAAP financial measure of real estate companies' operating performance and should not be considered an alternative to net income in accordance with GAAP or as a measure of liquidity. Same property NOI is considered by management to be an important operating performance measure frequently used by analysts and investors because it includes only the NOI of operating properties that have been owned and stabilized for the entire current and prior year reporting periods. Same property NOI assists in eliminating disparities due to the development, redevelopment, acquisition and disposition of properties during the periods presented and thus provides a more consistent performance measure for the comparison of the Company's properties. Same property NOI is calculated using rental property revenues (excluding straight-line rent adjustments, lease termination income, net, and amortization of above/below market rents), less charges for credit losses, operating and maintenance expenses, real estate taxes, and rent expenses, plus the Company's proportionate share of same property NOI from unconsolidated real estate joint ventures, calculated on the same basis. The Company's method of calculating same property NOI, which may differ from methods used by other REITs and may not be comparable to them, discloses with and without the impact from redevelopment projects.</font></p></td>
     </tr>
     <tr style="height:9pt;background-color:#ffffff;word-break:break-word;white-space:pre-wrap;text-align:left;">
      <td style="padding-top:0.01in;padding-left:0in;vertical-align:top;padding-bottom:0in;padding-right:0.01in;"><p style="tab-size:26.66px;font-size:9pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Arial;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">(2)</font></p></td>
      <td style="padding-top:0.01in;padding-left:0in;vertical-align:top;padding-bottom:0in;padding-right:0.01in;"><p style="tab-size:26.66px;font-size:9pt;margin-top:0;font-family:Arial;margin-bottom:0;text-align:left;"><font style="color:#000000;white-space:pre-wrap;font-kerning:none;min-width:fit-content;">Amounts represent the company's pro-rata share.</font></p></td>
     </tr>
